# Afternoon upstream delta dispositions, 2026-11-25

Source: [the afternoon delta](2026-11-25-afternoon-upstream-delta.md). Upstream diffs and file lists were read with `gh` on 2026-11-25.

## Coverage

**This pass does not disposition every row. 1076 open-PR rows remain pending and were not evaluated.**

The delta marks 1879 open-PR rows as changed or new, but it compared heads only with the morning delta and the November 25 reports. Matched against every earlier port report, 42 heads appear in none. Those 42 and all 23 OmniRoute commits were reviewed here, at the depth each row states.

| Open-PR rows | 9router | OmniRoute |
|---|---:|---:|
| Head in no earlier report: reviewed here | 4 | 38 |
| Same head has a recorded disposition: carried, not re-reviewed | 546 | 165 |
| Same head named in a report this pass did not parse: see that report | 42 | 8 |
| Pending: only an earlier head has a disposition | 31 | 33 |
| Pending: head found only in inventories | 704 | 308 |
| Total | 1327 | 552 |

Carried words come from a table cell of the linked report and were matched by script; a `split` or `deferred` result may leave work open. A report is read from its own table row, so the linked report stays authoritative. "See report" is not a disposition. Inventory rows marked pending, including [the November 24 census](2026-11-24-upstream-census.md), are not counted as recorded work.

## Ported in this pass

| Source | Behaviour | Commit |
|---|---|---|
| 9router #5860 | Claude 1h cache TTL kept on re-anchored breakpoints | `6070fb6c411` |
| OmniRoute #21148 | Header-safe client session ids | `70308b24501` |
| OmniRoute #21207 | Combo stream released after lifecycle progress | `43273cb3c06` |
| OmniRoute #21159 | No explicit checkpoint on SQLite lock release | `73fdddf79fb` |

Port candidates from this pass: 9router #5776 and #5702; OmniRoute #21221, #21216, #21057 and #21033.

The default branch has no new commits: the delta reports it identical to `5ae63d22847e07042e235b40e1d926b65e509013`.

### 9router open PRs: heads in no earlier report

| PR | Exact current head | Title | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|
| [#5860](https://github.com/decolua/9router/pull/5860) | [7ee6d9064ea332feaa6e449ea73e6789baee45de](https://github.com/decolua/9router/commit/7ee6d9064ea332feaa6e449ea73e6789baee45de) | fix(claude): keep the client's 1h TTL on re-anchored message breakpoints | **Ported.** Re-anchoring now gives the message breakpoint the TTL of the client's last message marker, in passthrough and translated requests. Upstream uses 1h if any message marker asks for it; the last marker is used here so the existing mixed-marker case keeps its 5m anchor. Requests that keep client markers (`preserveResponseCache`) stay at 5m. A 1h marker on a trailing system turn that folding drops is carried to the last user block. Commit `6070fb6c411`. [open-sse/translator/formats/claude.js](../../../open-sse/translator/formats/claude.js); test [tests/unit/port9r-claude-keep-client-ttl-5860.test.js](../../../tests/unit/port9r-claude-keep-client-ttl-5860.test.js). |
| [#5859](https://github.com/decolua/9router/pull/5859) | [a98b9d31e5d757a2a25aee271152421a6dd5b5c4](https://github.com/decolua/9router/commit/a98b9d31e5d757a2a25aee271152421a6dd5b5c4) | feat(auth): add soonest-reset account strategy | **Not applicable.** New upstream account-selection strategy (a product feature); `src/sse/services/quotaResetOrder.js` is absent. |
| [#5776](https://github.com/decolua/9router/pull/5776) | [f663037a82c7ada99bd4a02b745ea0f9e7761564](https://github.com/decolua/9router/commit/f663037a82c7ada99bd4a02b745ea0f9e7761564) | fix(opencode): sync free-lane client with OpenCode v2.3.2 | **Port candidate.** The only change since the recorded head `9d8614fad501171aeb2a7a390d025848c574965b` raises the OpenCode client User-Agent from 2.3.2 to 2.4.1. Gap: [open-sse/executors/opencode.js](../../../open-sse/executors/opencode.js) still sends 2.3.2. Not changed: no evidence here that the provider requires it, and [1072-opencode-free-client-5776.md](1072-opencode-free-client-5776.md) limits client-fingerprint changes. |
| [#5702](https://github.com/decolua/9router/pull/5702) | [37ff0d9899fc7a39e02bf1c7ea9687e5c0c97580](https://github.com/decolua/9router/commit/37ff0d9899fc7a39e02bf1c7ea9687e5c0c97580) | Rebase fork changes onto v0.5.119 | **Port candidate.** Not evaluated.
